routines\n   140→def bin_spikes(spike_times_list, go_times):\n   141→    \"\"\"Spike counts -> firing rate (Hz) in non-overlapping BIN_SIZE bins around the go cue.\n   142→\n   143→    Analogue of the reference `sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)` with stride == bin width.\n   144→\n   145→    Args:\n   146→        spike_times_list: list of (n_spikes,) sorted arrays, one per neuron (session clock).\n   147→        go_times: (n_trials,) go-cue times in session clock.\n   148→    Returns:\n   149→        (n_neurons, n_trials, NBINS) float32 firing rates in Hz.\n   150→    \"\"\"\n   151→    ntr = len(go_times)\n   152→    edges = (go_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES_REL[None, :]).ravel()\n   153→    out = np.empty((len(spike_times_list), ntr, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   154→    for k, st in enumerate(spike_times_list):\n   155→        idx = np.searchsorted(st, edges).reshape(ntr, NBINS + 1)\n   156→        out[k] = np.diff(idx, axis=1)\n   157→    out /= np.float32(BIN_SIZE)\n   158→    return out\n   159→\n   160→\n   161→def tone_onset_times(sample_starts, trial_starts, go_times):\n   162→    \"\"\"Last sample(tone)-epoch start at or before the go cue, for each trial.\n   163→\n   164→    Early licking triggers a replay of the sample/delay epoch, so a trial can contain several\n   165→    `sample_start_times`; the *last* one before the go cue is the tone the animal responded to.\n   166→    \"\"\"\n   167→    ntr = len(go_times)\n   168→    out = np.full(ntr, np.nan)\n   169→    if len(sample_starts):\n   170→        tidx = np.searchsorted(trial_starts, sample_starts, side='right') - 1\n   171→        ok = (tidx >= 0) & (tidx < ntr)\n   172→        tidx, ev = tidx[ok], sample_starts[ok]\n   173→        ok2 = ev <= go_times[tidx]\n   174→        tidx, ev = tidx[ok2], ev[ok2]\n   175→        order = np.argsort(ev, kind='stable')          # ascending -> last write wins\n   176→        out[tidx[order]] = ev[order]\n   177→    return out\n   178→\n   179→\n   180→def photostim_binary(stim_on, stim_off, trial_starts, go_times):\n   181→    \"\"\"(n_trials, NBINS) binary array: is photostimulation on during this bin?\"\"\"\n   182→    ntr = len(go_times)\n   183→    ps = np.zeros((ntr, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   184→    if len(stim_on) == 0:\n   185→        return ps\n   186→    tidx = np.searchsorted(trial_starts, stim_on, side='right') - 1\n   187→    for i, on, off in zip(tidx, stim_on, stim_off):\n   188→        if i < 0 or i >= ntr:\n   189→            continue\n   190→        on_rel, off_rel = on - go_times[i], off - go_times[i]\n   191→        if off_rel < on_rel:                      # guard against corrupt entries\n   192→            on_rel, off_rel = off_rel, on_rel\n   193→        # a bin is 'on' if the stimulation interval overlaps the bin\n   194→        overlap = (BIN_EDGES_REL[:-1] < off_rel) & (BIN_EDGES_REL[1:] > on_rel)\n   195→        ps[i, overlap] = 1.0\n   196→    return ps\n   197→\n   198→\n   199→def bin_tongue(ts, y, lik, go_times):\n   200→    \"\"\"Mean visible tongue y-position per 50 ms bin, NaN where the tongue is not visible.\n   201→\n   202→    DeepLabCut side-camera tracking (`Camera0_side_TongueTracking`), aligned to the go cue as in\n   203→    Sherlock/align_markers.py.  Following the method paper, frames with a 5-sigma velocity\n   204→    outlier are discarded; unlike the method paper (which imputes the mean for occluded frames)\n   205→    the decoder task asks for an explicit 'not visible' class, so bins without a valid visible\n   206→    frame are left as NaN and encoded as class 3 downstream.\n   207→    \"\"\"\n   208→    ntr = len(go_times)\n   209→    visible = lik > TONGUE_LIK_THRESH\n   210→    valid = visible.copy()\n   211→    if visible.sum() > 10:\n   212→        iv = np.where(visible)[0]\n   213→        dy = np.diff(y[iv]) / np.maximum(np.diff(ts[iv]), 1e-6)\n   214→        s = np.std(dy)\n   215→        if s > 0:\n   216→            bad = np.abs(dy) > VELOCITY_SIGMA * s\n   217→            # a large jump invalidates the frame after the jump\n   218→            valid[iv[1:][bad]] = False\n   219→    res = np.full((ntr, NBINS), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   220→    t0 = go_times + OFF_START\n   221→    lo = np.searchsorted(ts, t0)\n   222→    hi = np.searchsorted(ts, go_times + OFF_END)\n   223→    for i in range(ntr):\n   224→        a, b = lo[i], hi[i]\n   225→        if b <= a:\n   226→            continue\n   227→        tsel = ts[a:b]\n   228→        vsel = valid[a:b]\n   229→        if not vsel.any():\n   230→            continue\n   231→        bi = np.floor((tsel[vsel] - t0[i]) / BIN_SIZE).astype(int)\n   232→        np.clip(bi, 0, NBINS - 1, out=bi)\n   233→        cnt = np.bincount(bi, minlength=NBINS)\n   234→        sm = np.bincount(bi, weights=y[a:b][vsel], minlength=NBINS)\n   235→        nz = cnt > 0\n   236→        res[i, nz] = (sm[nz] / cnt[nz]).astype(np.float32)\n   237→    return res\n   238→\n   239→\n   240→def discretize_tongue(y_binned):\n   241→    \"\"\"Per-session discretisation of tongue y into 4 classes (task specification).\"\"\"\n   242→    vis = np.isfinite(y_binned)\n   243→    cls = np.full(y_binned.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)     # 3 = not visible\n   244→    if vis.sum() > 0:\n   245→        p40, p60 = np.percentile(y_binned[vis], [40, 60])\n   246→        v = y_binned[vis]\n   247→        c = np.where(v < p40, 0, np.where(v <= p60, 1, 2))\n   248→        cls[vis] = c\n   249→    else:\n   250→        p40 = p60 = np.nan\n   251→    return cls, float(p40), float(p60)\n   252→\n   253→\n   254→# ------------------------------------------------------------------- per-session conversion\n   255→def convert_session(path, show_processing=False):\n   256→    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session file; returns a dict or None if the session is dropped.\"\"\"\n   257→    t_start = time.time()\n   258→    timing = {}\n   259→    with NWBHDF5IO(path, mode='r', load_namespaces=True) as io:\n   260→        nwb = io.read()\n   261→        ident = nwb.identifier\n   262→        subject = str(nwb.subject.subject_id)\n   263→        subject_name = str(nwb.subject.description)\n   264→\n   265→        # ---------------- units: QC-classifier 'good' units only (reference neuron curation)\n   266→        t0 = time.time()\n   267→        units = nwb.units\n   268→        classification = np.asarray(units['classification'][:]).astype(str)\n   269→        good = np.where(classification == 'good')[0]\n   270→        if len(good) == 0:\n   271→            return {'identifier': ident, 'dropped': 'no good units'}\n   272→        anno = np.asarray(units['anno_name'][:]).astype(str)[good]\n   273→        # CCF coordinates of each unit's first electrode (fast VectorIndex access)\n   274→        vi = units['electrodes']\n   275→        ends = np.asarray(vi.data[:])\n   276→        starts = np.concatenate([[0], ends[:-1]])\n   277→        elec_ids = np.asarray(vi.target.data[:])[starts][good]\n   278→        edf = nwb.electrodes.to_dataframe()\n   279→        ccf_x = edf['x'].values.astype(float)[elec_ids]\n   280→        ccf_y = edf['y'].values.astype(float)[elec_ids]\n   281→        ccf_z = edf['z'].values.astype(float)[elec_ids]\n   282→        side = np.where(ccf_x >= ML_MIDLINE, 'left', 'right')   # reference midline convention\n   283→        groups = np.array([map_annotation(a) for a in anno])\n   284→        region_labels = np.array(['%s %s' % (s, g) for s, g in zip(side, groups)])\n   285→        timing['units_meta'] = time.time() - t0\n   286→\n   287→        # ---------------- trials\n   288→        t0 = time.time()\n   289→        tr = nwb.trials\n   290→        trial_start = np.asarray(tr['start_time'][:], dtype=float)\n   291→        trial_stop = np.asarray(tr['stop_time'][:], dtype=float)\n   292→        outcome = np.asarray(tr['outcome'][:]).astype(str)\n   293→        instruction = np.asarray(tr['trial_instruction'][:]).astype(str)\n   294→        early = np.asarray(tr['early_lick'][:]).astype(str)\n   295→        auto_water = np.asarray(tr['auto_water'][:]).astype(int)\n   296→        free_water = np.asarray(tr['free_water'][:]).astype(int)\n   297→        stim_flag_tbl = np.asarray(tr['photostim_power'][:]).astype(str) != 'N/A'\n   298→\n   299→        be = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents'].time_series\n   300→        go = np.asarray(be['go_start_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n   301→        sample_starts = np.asarray(be['sample_start_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n   302→        stim_on = np.asarray(be['photostim_start_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n   303→        stim_off = np.asarray(be['photostim_stop_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n   304→        assert len(go) == len(trial_start), 'go cue count != trial count in %s' % ident\n   305→        timing['trials'] = time.time() - t0\n   306→\n   307→        # trial curation: drop auto-water / free-water trials (reference get_regular_trial_mask);\n   308→        # early-lick, ignore/miss and photostim trials are KEPT because they are decoder outputs\n   309→        # / inputs in this task.\n   310→        keep = (auto_water == 0) & (free_water == 0)\n   311→        n_dropped_water = int((~keep).sum())\n   312→        kidx = np.where(keep)[0]\n   313→        ntr = len(kidx)\n   314→        if ntr < 2:\n   315→            return {'identifier': ident, 'dropped': 'fewer than 2 usable trials'}\n   316→\n   317→        # ---------------- neural: spikes -> firing rates aligned to the go cue\n   318→        t0 = time.time()\n   319→        spike_lists = [np.asarray(units['spike_times'][int(i)]) for i in good]\n   320→        timing['spike_read'] = time.time() - t0\n   321→        t0 = time.time()\n   322→        fr = bin_spikes(spike_lists, go[kidx])                    # (n_neurons, ntr, NBINS)\n   323→        timing['binning'] = time.time() - t0\n   324→\n   325→        # --- exclude trials with no ephys at all (acquisition gaps).\n   326→        # In some sessions the probes stop before the behaviour does, or single trials are\n   327→        # missing from the ephys stream; such trials contain zero spikes across *every* good\n   328→        # unit in the whole analysis window.  They carry no neural information and would be\n   329→        # pure noise for the decoder, so they are treated as invalid data periods and removed.\n   330→        spikes_per_trial = fr.sum(axis=(0, 2))\n   331→        rec = spikes_per_trial > 0\n   332→        n_dropped_norec = int((~rec).sum())\n   333→        if n_dropped_norec:\n   334→            fr = fr[:, rec, :]\n   335→            kidx = kidx[rec]\n   336→            ntr = int(rec.sum())\n   337→        if ntr < 2:\n   338→            return {'identifier': ident, 'dropped': 'fewer than 2 trials with ephys'}\n   339→\n   340→        # ---------------- inputs\n   341→        t0 = time.time()\n   342→        tone = tone_onset_times(sample_starts, trial_start, go)[kidx]\n   343→        tone_rel_go = tone - go[kidx]                             # negative, ~ -1.85 s\n   344→        # time since tone onset at each bin centre (s)\n   345→        time_from_tone = (BIN_CENTERS_REL[None, :] - tone_rel_go[:, None]).astype(np.float32)\n   346→        ps_all = photostim_binary(stim_on, stim_off, trial_start, go)\n   347→        photostim = ps_all[kidx]\n   348→        timing['inputs'] = time.time() - t0\n   349→\n   350→        # ---------------- outputs\n   351→        t0 = time.time()\n   352→        out_k = outcome[kidx]\n   353→        instr_k = instruction[kidx]\n   354→        # choice: hit -> instructed side, miss -> opposite side, ignore -> no lick\n   355→        choice = np.where(out_k == 'ignore', 2,\n   356→                          np.where(out_k == 'hit',\n   357→                                   np.where(instr_k == 'left', 0, 1),\n   358→                                   np.where(instr_k == 'left', 1, 0))).astype(np.int64)\n   359→        outcome_code = np.where(out_k == 'ignore', 0, np.where(out_k == 'miss', 1, 2)).astype(np.int64)\n   360→        early_code = (early[kidx] == 'early').astype(np.int64)\n   361→\n   362→        bts = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series\n   363→        tongue = bts['Camera0_side_TongueTracking']\n   364→        tt = np.asarray(tongue.timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n   365→        tdata = np.asarray(tongue.data[:], dtype=float)\n   366→        ty_binned = bin_tongue(tt, tdata[:, 1], tdata[:, 2], go[kidx])\n   367→        tongue_cls, p40, p60 = discretize_tongue(ty_binned)\n   368→        timing['outputs'] = time.time() - t0\n   369→\n   370→        # ---------------- assemble per-trial arrays\n   371→        neural_trials = [np.ascontiguousarray(fr[:, i, :]) for i in range(ntr)]\n   372→        input_trials = [np.stack([time_from_tone[i], photostim[i]]).astype(np.float32)\n   373→                        for i in range(ntr)]\n   374→        output_trials = [np.stack([np.full(NBINS, choice[i]),\n   375→                                   np.full(NBINS, outcome_code[i]),\n   376→                                   np.full(NBINS, early_code[i]),\n   377→                                   tongue_cls[i]]).astype(np.int64) for i in range(ntr)]\n   378→\n   379→        # ---------------- bookkeeping / diagnostics\n   380→        obs_frac_start = float(np.mean(trial_start[kidx] - go[kidx] <= OFF_START))\n   381→        obs_frac_end = float(np.mean(trial_stop[kidx] - go[kidx] >= OFF_END))\n   382→        stim_flag_evt = photostim.max(axis=1) > 0\n   383→        stim_mismatch = int(np.sum(stim_flag_evt != stim_flag_tbl[kidx]))\n   384→\n   385→        info = {\n   386→            'identifier': ident,\n   387→            'subject': subject,\n   388→            'subject_name': subject_name,\n   389→            'session_start_time': str(nwb.session_start_time),\n   390→            'n_neurons': int(len(good)),\n   391→            'n_units_total': int(len(classification)),\n   392→            'n_trials': int(ntr),\n   393→            'n_trials_raw': int(len(trial_start)),\n   394→            'n_trials_dropped_water': n_dropped_water,\n   395→            'n_trials_dropped_no_ephys': n_dropped_norec,\n   396→            'frac_trials_observed_at_start': obs_frac_start,\n   397→            'frac_trials_observed_at_end': obs_frac_end,\n   398→            'tongue_p40': p40, 'tongue_p60': p60,\n   399→            'frac_bins_tongue_visible': float(np.mean(np.isfinite(ty_binned))),\n   400→            'median_tone_onset_re_go': float(np.nanmedian(tone_rel_go)),\n   401→            'n_photostim_trials': int(stim_flag_evt.sum()),\n   402→            'photostim_table_mismatch': stim_mismatch,\n   403→            'timing': timing,\n   404→        }\n   405→\n   406→        result = {\n   407→            'identifier': ident,\n   408→            'subject': subject,\n   409→            'neural': neural_trials,\n   410→            'input': input_trials,\n   411→            'output': output_trials,\n   412→            'region_labels': region_labels,\n   413→            'ccf': np.stack([ccf_x, ccf_y, ccf_z], axis=1).astype(np.float32),\n   414→            'anno': anno,\n   415→            'info': info,\n   416→        }\n   417→\n   418→        if show_processing:\n   419→            make_processing_plots(ident, nwb, kidx, go, trial_start, trial_stop, tone,\n   420→                                  spike_lists, fr, time_from_tone, photostim, tt, tdata,\n   421→                                  ty_binned, tongue_cls, p40, p60, choice, outcome_code,\n   422→                                  early_code, region_labels)\n   423→        info['total_time'] = time.time() - t_start\n   424→        return result\n   425→\n   426→\n   427→# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ diagnostics plots\n   428→def make_processing_plots(ident, nwb, kidx, go, trial_start, trial_stop, tone, spike_lists,\n   429→                          fr, time_from_tone, photostim, tt, tdata, ty_binned, tongue_cls,\n   430→                          p40, p60, choice, outcome_code, early_code, region_labels):\n   431→    import matplotlib\n   432→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   433→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   434→\n   435→    ntr = len(kidx)\n   436→    stim_trials = np.where(photostim.max(axis=1) > 0)[0]\n   437→    itrial = int(stim_trials[0]) if len(stim_trials) else 0\n   438→    gidx = kidx[itrial]\n   439→    g = go[gidx]\n   440→\n   441→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(20, 18))\n   442→\n   443→    # (0,0) raster of raw spikes for one trial vs binned rates\n   444→    a = ax[0, 0]\n   445→    nshow = min(40, len(spike_lists))\n   446→    for k in range(nshow):\n   447→        s = spike_lists[k]\n   448→        s = s[(s >= g + OFF_START) & (s < g + OFF_END)] - g\n   449→        a.plot(s, np.full(len(s), k), '|', color='k', ms=3)\n   450→    a.axvline(0, color='r', label='go cue')\n   451→    a.axvline(tone[itrial] - g, color='g', label='tone onset')\n   452→    a.axvline(trial_start[gidx] - g, color='b', ls='--', label='trial start')\n   453→    a.axvline(trial_stop[gidx] - g, color='b', ls=':', label='trial stop')\n   454→    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='neuron', title='%s trial %d: raw spikes' % (ident, itrial))\n   455→    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n   456→\n   457→    # (0,1) binned rates of the same neurons (image) -- checks alignment/binning\n   458→    a = ax[0, 1]\n   459→    im = a.imshow(fr[:nshow, itrial, :], aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n   460→                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, nshow], cmap='magma')\n   461→    a.axvline(0, color='r'); a.axvline(tone[itrial] - g, color='g')\n   462→    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='neuron', title='binned firing rate (Hz), same trial')\n   463→    plt.colorbar(im, ax=a)\n   464→\n   465→    # (1,0) population PSTH: raw spike histogram vs converted rates\n   466→    a = ax[1, 0]\n   467→    raw = np.zeros(NBINS)\n   468→    sub = min(60, len(spike_lists))\n   469→    for k in range(sub):\n   470→        s = spike_lists[k]\n   471→        for i in kidx[:200]:\n   472→            m = s[(s >= go[i] + OFF_START) & (s < go[i] + OFF_END)] - go[i]\n   473→            raw += np.histogram(m, bins=BIN_EDGES_REL)[0]\n   474→    raw = raw / (min(200, ntr) * sub * BIN_SIZE)\n   475→    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, raw, 'k', label='independent histogram of raw spikes')\n   476→    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, fr[:sub, :200, :].mean(axis=(0, 1)), 'r--', label='converted neural mean')\n   477→    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3); a.axvline(-1.85, color='g', alpha=.3)\n   478→    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='rate (Hz)', title='population PSTH sanity check')\n   479→    a.legend(fontsize=8)\n   480→\n   481→    # (1,1) inputs for the example trial\n   482→    a = ax[1, 1]\n   483→    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, time_from_tone[itrial], label='input0: time from tone onset (s)')\n   484→    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, photostim[itrial], label='input1: photostim on')\n   485→    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3, label='go cue')\n   486→    a.axhline(0, color='grey', lw=.5)\n   487→    a.axvline(tone[itrial] - g, color='g', alpha=.5, label='tone onset (raw)')\n   488→    be = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents'].time_series\n   489→    son = np.asarray(be['photostim_start_times'].timestamps[:])\n   490→    sof = np.asarray(be['photostim_stop_times'].timestamps[:])\n   491→    m = (son >= trial_start[gidx]) & (son <= trial_stop[gidx])\n   492→    for s0, s1 in zip(son[m] - g, sof[m] - g):\n   493→        a.axvspan(s0, s1, color='orange', alpha=.3, label='raw photostim interval')\n   494→    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', title='inputs, trial %d' % itrial)\n   495→    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n   496→\n   497→    # (2,0) tongue tracking raw vs binned for the example trial\n   498→    a = ax[2, 0]\n   499→    m = (tt >= g + OFF_START) & (tt < g + OFF_END)\n   500→    vis = tdata[m, 2] > TONGUE_LIK_THRESH\n   501→    a.plot(tt[m] - g, np.where(vis, tdata[m, 1], np.nan), '.', ms=2, label='raw tongue y (visible)')\n   502→    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, ty_binned[itrial], 'r.-', label='binned mean y')\n   503→    a.axhline(p40, color='g', ls='--', label='40th pct')\n   504→    a.axhline(p60, color='b', ls='--', label='60th 508→\n   509→    # (2,1) resulting tongue classes\n   510→    a = ax[2, 1]\n   511→    a.step(BIN_CENTERS_REL, tongue_cls[itrial], where='mid', label='tongue class')\n   512→    a.set(xlabel='time re a.legend(fontsize=7)\n   516→\n   517→    # (3,0) per-trial outputs across the session\n   518→    a = ax[3, 0]\n   519→    a.plot(choice, '.', label='choice (0 L, 1 R, 2 none)')\n   520→    a.plot(outcome_code + 3.2, '.', label='outcome+3.2 (0 ign, 1 miss, 2 hit)')\n   521→    a.plot(early_code + 6.4, '.', label='early lick+6.4')\n   522→    a.set(xlabel='trial', title='per-trial outputs')\n   523→    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n   524→\n   525→    # (3,1) tongue class fractions over time + region composition\n   526→    a = ax[3, 1]\n   527→    for c, lab in enumerate(OUTPUT_VALUES[3]):\n   528→        a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, np.mean(tongue_cls == c, axis=0), label=lab)\n   529→    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3)\n   530→    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='fraction of trials', title='tongue class vs time')\n   531→    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n   532→\n   533→    fig.suptitle('Processing diagnostics: %s (%d neurons, %d trials, regions: %s)' map_annotation(name):\n    \"\"\"Map a fine CCF annotation string to one of the 14 coarse reference groups.\"\"\"\n    n = str(name).strip()\n    for group, terms in REGION_RULES:\n        for t in terms:\n            if t in n:\n                return group\n    if any(k in n for k in CORTEX_KEYWORDS):\n        return 'OtherCortex'\n    return 'Unknown'\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ helper routines\ndef bin_spikes(spike_times_list, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Spike counts -> firing rate (Hz) in non-overlapping BIN_SIZE bins around the go cue.\n\n    Analogue of the reference `sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)` with stride == bin width.\n\n    Args:\n        spike_times_list: list of (n_spikes,) sorted arrays, one per neuron (session clock).\n        go_times: (n_trials,) go-cue times in session clock.\n    Returns:\n        (n_neurons, n_trials, NBINS) float32 firing rates in Hz.\n    \"\"\"\n    ntr = len(go_times)\n    edges = (go_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES_REL[None, :]).ravel()\n    out = np.empty((len(spike_times_list), ntr, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    for k, st in enumerate(spike_times_list):\n        idx = np.searchsorted(st, edges).reshape(ntr, NBINS + 1)\n        out[k] = np.diff(idx, axis=1)\n    out /= np.float32(BIN_SIZE)\n    return out\n\n\ndef tone_onset_times(sample_starts, trial_starts, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Last sample(tone)-epoch start at or before the go cue, for each trial.\n\n    Early licking triggers a replay of the sample/delay epoch, so a trial can contain several\n    `sample_start_times`; the *last* one before the go cue is the tone the animal responded to.\n    \"\"\"\n    ntr = len(go_times)\n    out = np.full(ntr, np.nan)\n    if len(sample_starts):\n        tidx = np.searchsorted(trial_starts, sample_starts, side='right') - 1\n        ok = (tidx >= 0) & (tidx < ntr)\n        tidx, ev = tidx[ok], sample_starts[ok]\n        ok2 = ev <= go_times[tidx]\n        tidx, ev = tidx[ok2], ev[ok2]\n        order = np.argsort(ev, kind='stable')          # ascending -> last write wins\n        out[tidx[order]] = ev[order]\n    return out\n\n\ndef photostim_binary(stim_on, stim_off, trial_starts, go_times):\n    \"\"\"(n_trials, NBINS) binary array: is photostimulation on during this bin?\"\"\"\n    ntr = len(go_times)\n    ps = np.zeros((ntr, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    if len(stim_on) == 0:\n        return ps\n    tidx = np.searchsorted(trial_starts, stim_on, side='right') - 1\n    for i, on, off in zip(tidx, stim_on, stim_off):\n        if i < 0 or i >= ntr:\n            continue\n        on_rel, off_rel = on - go_times[i], off - go_times[i]\n        if off_rel < on_rel:                      # guard against corrupt entries\n            on_rel, off_rel = off_rel, on_rel\n        # a bin is 'on' if the stimulation interval overlaps the bin\n        overlap = (BIN_EDGES_REL[:-1] < off_rel) & (BIN_EDGES_REL[1:] > on_rel)\n        ps[i, overlap] = 1.0\n    return ps\n\n\ndef bin_tongue(ts, y, lik, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Mean visible tongue y-position per 50 ms bin, NaN where the tongue is not visible.\n\n    DeepLabCut side-camera tracking (`Camera0_side_TongueTracking`), aligned to the go cue as in\n    Sherlock/align_markers.py.  Following the method paper, frames with a 5-sigma velocity\n    outlier are discarded; unlike the method paper (which imputes the mean for occluded frames)\n    the decoder task asks for an explicit 'not visible' class, so bins without a valid visible\n    frame are left as NaN and encoded as class 3 downstream.\n    \"\"\"\n    ntr = len(go_times)\n    visible = lik > TONGUE_LIK_THRESH\n    valid = visible.copy()\n    if visible.sum() > 10:\n        iv = np.where(visible)[0]\n        dy = np.diff(y[iv]) / np.maximum(np.diff(ts[iv]), 1e-6)\n        s = np.std(dy)\n        if s > 0:\n            bad = np.abs(dy) > VELOCITY_SIGMA * s\n            # a large jump invalidates the frame after the jump\n            valid[iv[1:][bad]] = False\n    res = np.full((ntr, NBINS), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n    t0 = go_times + OFF_START\n    lo = np.searchsorted(ts, t0)\n    hi = np.searchsorted(ts, go_times + OFF_END)\n    for i in range(ntr):\n        a, b = lo[i], hi[i]\n        if b <= a:\n            continue\n        tsel = ts[a:b]\n        vsel = valid[a:b]\n        if not vsel.any():\n            continue\n        bi = np.floor((tsel[vsel] - t0[i]) / BIN_SIZE).astype(int)\n        np.clip(bi, 0, NBINS - 1, out=bi)\n        cnt = np.bincount(bi, minlength=NBINS)\n        sm = np.bincount(bi, weights=y[a:b][vsel], minlength=NBINS)\n        nz = cnt > 0\n        res[i, nz] = (sm[nz] / cnt[nz]).astype(np.float32)\n    return res\n\n\ndef discretize_tongue(y_binned):\n    \"\"\"Per-session discretisation of tongue y into 4 classes (task specification).\"\"\"\n    vis = np.isfinite(y_binned)\n    cls = np.full(y_binned.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)     # 3 = not visible\n    if vis.sum() > 0:\n        p40, p60 = np.percentile(y_binned[vis], [40, 60])\n        v = y_binned[vis]\n        c = np.where(v < p40, 0, np.where(v <= p60, 1, 2))\n        cls[vis] = c\n    else:\n        p40 = p60 = np.nan\n    return cls, float(p40), float(p60)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- per-session conversion\ndef convert_session(path, show_processing=False):\n    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session file; returns a dict or None if the session is dropped.\"\"\"\n    t_start = time.time()\n    timing = {}\n    with NWBHDF5IO(path, mode='r', load_namespaces=True) as io:\n        nwb = io.read()\n        ident = nwb.identifier\n        subject = str(nwb.subject.subject_id)\n        subject_name = str(nwb.subject.description)\n\n        # ---------------- units: QC-classifier 'good' units only (reference neuron curation)\n        t0 = time.time()\n        units = nwb.units\n        classification = np.asarray(units['classification'][:]).astype(str)\n        good = np.where(classification == 'good')[0]\n        if len(good) == 0:\n            return {'identifier': ident, 'dropped': 'no good units'}\n        anno = np.asarray(units['anno_name'][:]).astype(str)[good]\n        # CCF coordinates of each unit's first electrode (fast VectorIndex access)\n        vi = units['electrodes']\n        ends = np.asarray(vi.data[:])\n        starts = np.concatenate([[0], ends[:-1]])\n        elec_ids = np.asarray(vi.target.data[:])[starts][good]\n        edf = nwb.electrodes.to_dataframe()\n        ccf_x = edf['x'].values.astype(float)[elec_ids]\n        ccf_y = edf['y'].values.astype(float)[elec_ids]\n        ccf_z = edf['z'].values.astype(float)[elec_ids]\n        side = np.where(ccf_x >= ML_MIDLINE, 'left', 'right')   # reference midline convention\n        groups = np.array([map_annotation(a) for a in anno])\n        region_labels = np.array(['%s %s' % (s, g) for s, g in zip(side, groups)])\n        timing['units_meta'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # ---------------- trials\n        t0 = time.time()\n        tr = nwb.trials\n        trial_start = np.asarray(tr['start_time'][:], dtype=float)\n        trial_stop = np.asarray(tr['stop_time'][:], dtype=float)\n        outcome = np.asarray(tr['outcome'][:]).astype(str)\n        instruction = np.asarray(tr['trial_instruction'][:]).astype(str)\n        early = np.asarray(tr['early_lick'][:]).astype(str)\n        auto_water = np.asarray(tr['auto_water'][:]).astype(int)\n        free_water = np.asarray(tr['free_water'][:]).astype(int)\n        stim_flag_tbl = np.asarray(tr['photostim_power'][:]).astype(str) != 'N/A'\n\n        be = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents'].time_series\n        go = np.asarray(be['go_start_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n        sample_starts = np.asarray(be['sample_start_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n        stim_on = np.asarray(be['photostim_start_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n        stim_off = np.asarray(be['photostim_stop_times'].timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n        assert len(go) == len(trial_start), 'go cue count != trial count in %s' % ident\n        timing['trials'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # trial curation: drop auto-water / free-water trials (reference get_regular_trial_mask);\n        # early-lick, ignore/miss and photostim trials are KEPT because they are decoder outputs\n        # / inputs in this task.\n        keep = (auto_water == 0) & (free_water == 0)\n        n_dropped_water = int((~keep).sum())\n        kidx = np.where(keep)[0]\n        ntr = len(kidx)\n        if ntr < 2:\n            return {'identifier': ident, 'dropped': 'fewer than 2 usable trials'}\n\n        # ---------------- neural: spikes -> firing rates aligned to the go cue\n        t0 = time.time()\n        spike_lists = [np.asarray(units['spike_times'][int(i)]) for i in good]\n        timing['spike_read'] = time.time() - t0\n        t0 = time.time()\n        fr = bin_spikes(spike_lists, go[kidx])                    # (n_neurons, ntr, NBINS)\n        timing['binning'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # --- exclude trials with no ephys at all (acquisition gaps).\n        # In some sessions the probes stop before the behaviour does, or single trials are\n        # missing from the ephys stream; such trials contain zero spikes across *every* good\n        # unit in the whole analysis window.  They carry no neural information and would be\n        # pure noise for the decoder, so they are treated as invalid data periods and removed.\n        spikes_per_trial = fr.sum(axis=(0, 2))\n        rec = spikes_per_trial > 0\n        n_dropped_norec = int((~rec).sum())\n        if n_dropped_norec:\n            fr = fr[:, rec, :]\n            kidx = kidx[rec]\n            ntr = int(rec.sum())\n        if ntr < 2:\n            return {'identifier': ident, 'dropped': 'fewer than 2 trials with ephys'}\n\n        # ---------------- inputs\n        t0 = time.time()\n        tone = tone_onset_times(sample_starts, trial_start, go)[kidx]\n        tone_rel_go = tone - go[kidx]                             # negative, ~ -1.85 s\n        # time since tone onset at each bin centre (s)\n        time_from_tone = (BIN_CENTERS_REL[None, :] - tone_rel_go[:, None]).astype(np.float32)\n        ps_all = photostim_binary(stim_on, stim_off, trial_start, go)\n        photostim = ps_all[kidx]\n        timing['inputs'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # ---------------- outputs\n        t0 = time.time()\n        out_k = outcome[kidx]\n        instr_k = instruction[kidx]\n        # choice: hit -> instructed side, miss -> opposite side, ignore -> no lick\n        choice = np.where(out_k == 'ignore', 2,\n                          np.where(out_k == 'hit',\n                                   np.where(instr_k == 'left', 0, 1),\n                                   np.where(instr_k == 'left', 1, 0))).astype(np.int64)\n        outcome_code = np.where(out_k == 'ignore', 0, np.where(out_k == 'miss', 1, 2)).astype(np.int64)\n        early_code = (early[kidx] == 'early').astype(np.int64)\n\n        bts = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series\n        tongue = bts['Camera0_side_TongueTracking']\n        tt = np.asarray(tongue.timestamps[:], dtype=float)\n        tdata = np.asarray(tongue.data[:], dtype=float)\n        ty_binned = bin_tongue(tt, tdata[:, 1], tdata[:, 2], go[kidx])\n        tongue_cls, p40, p60 = discretize_tongue(ty_binned)\n        timing['outputs'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # ---------------- assemble per-trial arrays\n        neural_trials = [np.ascontiguousarray(fr[:, i, :]) for i in range(ntr)]\n        input_trials = [np.stack([time_from_tone[i], photostim[i]]).astype(np.float32)\n                        for i in range(ntr)]\n        output_trials = [np.stack([np.full(NBINS, choice[i]),\n                                   np.full(NBINS, outcome_code[i]),\n                                   np.full(NBINS, early_code[i]),\n                                   tongue_cls[i]]).astype(np.int64) for i in range(ntr)]\n\n        # ---------------- bookkeeping / diagnostics\n        obs_frac_start = float(np.mean(trial_start[kidx] - go[kidx] <= OFF_START))\n        obs_frac_end = float(np.mean(trial_stop[kidx] - go[kidx] >= OFF_END))\n        stim_flag_evt = photostim.max(axis=1) > 0\n        stim_mismatch = int(np.sum(stim_flag_evt != stim_flag_tbl[kidx]))\n\n        info = {\n            'identifier': ident,\n            'subject': subject,\n            'subject_name': subject_name,\n            'session_start_time': str(nwb.session_start_time),\n            'n_neurons': int(len(good)),\n            'n_units_total': int(len(classification)),\n            'n_trials': int(ntr),\n            'n_trials_raw': int(len(trial_start)),\n            'n_trials_dropped_water': n_dropped_water,\n            'n_trials_dropped_no_ephys': n_dropped_norec,\n            'frac_trials_observed_at_start': obs_frac_start,\n            'frac_trials_observed_at_end': obs_frac_end,\n            'tongue_p40': p40, 'tongue_p60': p60,\n            'frac_bins_tongue_visible': float(np.mean(np.isfinite(ty_binned))),\n            'median_tone_onset_re_go': float(np.nanmedian(tone_rel_go)),\n            'n_photostim_trials': int(stim_flag_evt.sum()),\n            'photostim_table_mismatch': stim_mismatch,\n            'timing': timing,\n        }\n\n        result = {\n            'identifier': ident,\n            'subject': subject,\n            'neural': neural_trials,\n            'input': input_trials,\n            'output': output_trials,\n            'region_labels': region_labels,\n            'ccf': np.stack([ccf_x, ccf_y, ccf_z], axis=1).astype(np.float32),\n            'anno': anno,\n            'info': info,\n        }\n\n        if show_processing:\n            make_processing_plots(ident, nwb, kidx, go, trial_start, trial_stop, tone,\n                                  spike_lists, fr, time_from_tone, photostim, tt, tdata,\n                                  ty_binned, tongue_cls, p40, p60, choice, outcome_code,\n                                  early_code, region_labels)\n        info['total_time'] = time.time() - t_start\n        return result\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ diagnostics plots\ndef make_processing_plots(ident, nwb, kidx, go, trial_start, trial_stop, tone, spike_lists,\n                          fr, time_from_tone, photostim, tt, tdata, ty_binned, tongue_cls,\n                          p40, p60, choice, outcome_code, early_code, region_labels):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    ntr = len(kidx)\n    stim_trials = np.where(photostim.max(axis=1) > 0)[0]\n    itrial = int(stim_trials[0]) if len(stim_trials) else 0\n    gidx = kidx[itrial]\n    g = go[gidx]\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(20, 18))\n\n    # (0,0) raster of raw spikes for one trial vs binned rates\n    a = ax[0, 0]\n    nshow = min(40, len(spike_lists))\n    for k in range(nshow):\n        s = spike_lists[k]\n        s = s[(s >= g + OFF_START) & (s < g + OFF_END)] - g\n        a.plot(s, np.full(len(s), k), '|', color='k', ms=3)\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', label='go cue')\n    a.axvline(tone[itrial] - g, color='g', label='tone onset')\n    a.axvline(trial_start[gidx] - g, color='b', ls='--', label='trial start')\n    a.axvline(trial_stop[gidx] - g, color='b', ls=':', label='trial stop')\n    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='neuron', title='%s trial %d: raw spikes' % (ident, itrial))\n    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    # (0,1) binned rates of the same neurons (image) -- checks alignment/binning\n    a = ax[0, 1]\n    im = a.imshow(fr[:nshow, itrial, :], aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, nshow], cmap='magma')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r'); a.axvline(tone[itrial] - g, color='g')\n    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='neuron', title='binned firing rate (Hz), same trial')\n    plt.colorbar(im, ax=a)\n\n    # (1,0) population PSTH: raw spike histogram vs converted rates\n    a = ax[1, 0]\n    raw = np.zeros(NBINS)\n    sub = min(60, len(spike_lists))\n    for k in range(sub):\n        s = spike_lists[k]\n        for i in kidx[:200]:\n            m = s[(s >= go[i] + OFF_START) & (s < go[i] + OFF_END)] - go[i]\n            raw += np.histogram(m, bins=BIN_EDGES_REL)[0]\n    raw = raw / (min(200, ntr) * sub * BIN_SIZE)\n    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, raw, 'k', label='independent histogram of raw spikes')\n    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, fr[:sub, :200, :].mean(axis=(0, 1)), 'r--', label='converted neural mean')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3); a.axvline(-1.85, color='g', alpha=.3)\n    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='rate (Hz)', title='population PSTH sanity check')\n    a.legend(fontsize=8)\n\n    # (1,1) inputs for the example trial\n    a = ax[1, 1]\n    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, time_from_tone[itrial], label='input0: time from tone onset (s)')\n    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, photostim[itrial], label='input1: photostim on')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3, label='go cue')\n    a.axhline(0, color='grey', lw=.5)\n    a.axvline(tone[itrial] - g, color='g', alpha=.5, label='tone onset (raw)')\n    be = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents'].time_series\n    son = np.asarray(be['photostim_start_times'].timestamps[:])\n    sof = np.asarray(be['photostim_stop_times'].timestamps[:])\n    m = (son >= trial_start[gidx]) & (son <= trial_stop[gidx])\n    for s0, s1 in zip(son[m] - g, sof[m] - g):\n        a.axvspan(s0, s1, color='orange', alpha=.3, label='raw photostim interval')\n    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', title='inputs, trial %d' % itrial)\n    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    # (2,0) tongue tracking raw vs binned for the example trial\n    a = ax[2, 0]\n    m = (tt >= g + OFF_START) & (tt < g + OFF_END)\n    vis = tdata[m, 2] > TONGUE_LIK_THRESH\n    a.plot(tt[m] - g, np.where(vis, tdata[m, 1], np.nan), '.', ms=2, label='raw tongue y (visible)')\n    a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, ty_binned[itrial], 'r.-', label='binned mean y')\n    a.axhline(p40, color='g', ls='--', label='40th pct')\n    a.axhline(p60, color='b', ls='--', label='60th pct')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3)\n    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='y (px)', title='tongue tracking, trial %d' % itrial)\n    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    # (2,1) resulting tongue classes\n    a = ax[2, 1]\n    a.step(BIN_CENTERS_REL, tongue_cls[itrial], where='mid', label='tongue class')\n    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='class (0,1,2 = y pct; 3 = not visible)',\n          title='discretised tongue output', ylim=[-0.5, 3.5])\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3)\n    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    # (3,0) per-trial outputs across the session\n    a = ax[3, 0]\n    a.plot(choice, '.', label='choice (0 L, 1 R, 2 none)')\n    a.plot(outcome_code + 3.2, '.', label='outcome+3.2 (0 ign, 1 miss, 2 hit)')\n    a.plot(early_code + 6.4, '.', label='early lick+6.4')\n    a.set(xlabel='trial', title='per-trial outputs')\n    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    # (3,1) tongue class fractions over time + region composition\n    a = ax[3, 1]\n    for c, lab in enumerate(OUTPUT_VALUES[3]):\n        a.plot(BIN_CENTERS_REL, np.mean(tongue_cls == c, axis=0), label=lab)\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', alpha=.3)\n    a.set(xlabel='time re go cue (s)', ylabel='fraction of trials', title='tongue class vs time')\n    a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    fig.suptitle('Processing diagnostics: %s (%d neurons, %d trials, regions: %s)' %\n                 (ident, fr.shape[0], ntr, ', '.join(sorted(set(region_labels))[:6])))\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    fname = 'processing_%s.png' % ident\n    fig.savefig(fname, dpi=110)\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print('  wrote %s' % fname)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ main\ndef list_sessions():\n    import glob\n    return sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'sub-*', '*.nwb')))\n\n\ndef _worker(args):\n    path, show = args\n    try:\n        return convert_session(path, show_processing=show)\n    except Exception as e:  # pragma: no cover - defensive\n        import traceback\n        return {'identifier': os.path.basename(path), 'error': traceback.format_exc()}\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\n    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\n    ap.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=16)\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    files = list_sessions()\n    if args.sample:\n        files = files[:2]\n    print('Converting %d sessions -> %s' % (len(files), args.outfile))\n    t_all = time.time()\n\n    show_n = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n    tasks = [(f, i < show_n) for i, f in enumerate(files)]\n\n    results = []\n    if args.workers > 1 and len(files) > 1:\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(min(args.workers, len(files))) as ex:\n            for i, r in enumerate(ex.map(_worker, tasks)):\n                results.append(r)\n                if (i + 1) % 10 == 0 or i == len(files) - 1:\n                    print('  %3d/%d done (%.1f s elapsed)' % (i + 1, len(files), time.time() - t_all))\n    else:\n        for i, t in enumerate(tasks):\n            results.append(_worker(t))\n            print('  %d/%d %s (%.1f s)' % (i + 1, len(files), results[-1].get('identifier'),\n                                           time.time() - t_all))\n\n    errors = [r for r in results if 'error' in r]\n    for r in errors:\n        print('ERROR in %s:\\n%s' % (r['identifier'], r['error']))\n    dropped = [r for r in results if r.get('dropped')]\n    for r in dropped:\n        print('DROPPED %s: %s' % (r['identifier'], r['dropped']))\n    good = [r for r in results if 'neural' in r]\n    print('Converted %d sessions (%d dropped, %d errors)' % (len(good), len(dropped), len(errors)))\n\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in good})\n    subject_idx = np.array([subjects.index(r['subject']) for r in good], dtype=np.int64)\n    brain_regions = sorted({lab for r in good for lab in r['region_labels']})\n    region_index = {lab: i for i, lab in enumerate(brain_regions)}\n    brain_region_idx = [np.array([region_index[l] for l in r['region_labels']], dtype=np.int64)\n                        for r in good]\n\n    session_info = [r['info'] for r in good]\n    n_neurons = int(sum(i['n_neurons'] for i in session_info))\n    n_trials = int(sum(i['n_trials'] for i in session_info))\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in good],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in good],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in good],\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': subject_idx,\n        'brain_regions': brain_regions,\n        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx,\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n        'metadata': {\n            'dataset': 'Mesoscale Activity Map (DANDI:000363), Chen et al. 2024 Cell; '\n                       'analysed in Wang*, Kurgyis* et al. 2025 Nat Neurosci',\n            'task_description': (\n                'Head-fixed mice perform an auditory delayed-response (memory-guided movement) '\n                'task. A tone (3 kHz or 12 kHz, three 150 ms pips) instructs lick-left or '\n                'lick-right; after a 1.2 s delay an auditory go cue (6 kHz, 0.1 s) permits the '\n                'response. Outputs decoded from neural activity: lick direction choice '\n                '(left/right/no lick), trial outcome (ignore/miss/hit), early lick (no/yes) and '\n                'discretised tongue y-position from side-view DeepLabCut tracking. Decoder '\n                'inputs: time since tone (sample epoch) onset and whether ALM photoinhibition '\n                'is on.'),\n            'time_bin_size': BIN_SIZE * 1000.0,\n            'time_bin_size_units': 'ms',\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'auditory go cue onset (BehavioralEvents go_start_times)',\n            'off_start': OFF_START,\n            'off_end': OFF_END,\n            'bin_centers_re_go_cue_s': BIN_CENTERS_REL.tolist(),\n            'neural_units': 'firing rate (Hz), spike counts per 50 ms bin / 0.05 s',\n            'n_sessions': len(good),\n            'n_subjects': len(subjects),\n            'n_neurons_total': n_neurons,\n            'n_trials_total': n_trials,\n            'neuron_curation': (\n                \"units with units.classification == 'good' (output of the region-specific QC \"\n                'classifiers of Chen, Liu et al. 2023 white paper, the same good-unit lists used '\n                'by the reference pipeline). These units also all carry a CCF annotation, which '\n                \"reproduces the reference's requirement of joint ephys+histology.\"),\n            'trial_curation': (\n                'trials with zero spikes from every good unit across the whole analysis window \\n'\n                '(ephys acquisition gaps) removed; auto-water and free-water trials removed (reward independent of the action, as '\n                'in the reference get_regular_trial_mask). Early-lick, no-response (ignore), '\n                'error (miss) and photostimulation trials are KEPT because the decoder task '\n                'defines them as outputs/inputs.'),\n            'session_curation': 'sessions without any good unit are dropped (1 session).',\n            'tongue_discretisation': (\n                'per-session percentiles (40th, 60th) of the binned, visible tongue y-position; '\n                'bins with no frame of likelihood > 0.5 (or only 5-sigma velocity outliers) are '\n                \"class 3 = 'not visible'\"),\n            'video_tracking': 'DeepLabCut side-view camera (Camera0_side_TongueTracking), 3.4 ms frames',\n            'notes_unobserved_time': (\n                'spikes are recorded only inside each trial interval (units.obs_intervals == '\n                'trials start/stop); 96.9 % of trials cover go-2.5 s and 84.4 % cover go+1.5 s '\n                '(error/miss trials terminate early). Bins outside the recorded interval contain '\n                'zero spikes by construction; per-session coverage is reported in session_info.'),\n            'session_info': session_info,\n        },\n    }\n\n    print('Writing pickle ...')\n    t0 = time.time()\n    with open(args.outfile, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=4)\n    print('wrote %s (%.2f GB) in %.1f s' % for our task we are instructed to use 50-ms bins, so we will use non-overlapping 50-ms bins (stride = width = 50 ms), which is the natural analogue.\n    47→- Epochs used in the paper (relative to go cue): sample `[-1.85, -1.2]` s, delay `[-1.2, 0]` s, response `[0, 1.5]` s. So the trial structure is ~1.3 s sample + 1.2 s delay; our window of -2.5 to +1.5 s around the go cue covers pre-sample, sample, delay and response.\n    48→- Neuron QC in the reference is performed with an **external classifier-based good-unit list** (`goodunits/*.mat`) not shipped with the NWB data. The NWB `units` table (DANDI 000363) contains per-unit QC metrics (`unit_amp`, `presence_ratio`, `amplitude_cutoff`, `isi_violation`, `drift_metric`, `unit_quality`...) and typically a `unit_quality`/`quality` field; we will reproduce the QC using the criteria in the Chen/Liu et al. 2023 spike-sorting QC white paper (to be read in Step 3) applied to those metrics.\n    49→- Units without CCF histology annotation are dropped in the reference (`unit_comb = set(ephys unit ids) & set(histology unit ids)`).\n    50→- Hemisphere assignment: CCF ML midline at 5700 µm; `ccf_x >= 5700` = left hemisphere.\n    51→- Trial curation for the reference **regression** analyses excludes early-lick / auto-water / free-water / no-response / photostim trials. Our decoder task explicitly requires photostim as an input and early lick, no-lick choice and ignore/miss outcome as outputs, so those trials must be **kept**; we will only exclude auto-water/free-water trials (not real behaviour) if warranted, documented in Step 5.\n    52→\n    53→---\n    54→\n    55→## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n    56→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    57→\n    58→### Data Structure\n    59→- `/app/data/` contains one directory per mouse: `sub-<subject_id>/` (28 subjects) and `dandiset.yaml` (DANDI:000363, Mesoscale Activity Map dataset, Chen et al.).\n    60→- Each session is one NWB file: `sub-<id>_ses-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSS>_behavior+ecephys+ogen.nwb`; Each has `data` of shape (n_frames, 3) = (x, y, likelihood) and explicit `timestamps` in session time with **dt = 0.0034 s** (identical in all 174 sessions), segmented per trial: each trial's video segment starts exactly at that trial's `start_time`.\n    70→- `nwb.units`: 39 columns, incl. `spike_times` (session clock), `obs_intervals` (= exactly the (start_time, stop_time) of every trial → **spikes are only recorded within trials**), `unit_quality` (`good`/`multi`, Kilosort-level), **`classification`** (`good`/`unlabelled`, output of the paper's QC classifier), **`anno_name`** (fine CCF annotation string, empty for `unlabelled` units), `is_good_trials` (bool per trial; `trial stop - go`: median ≈ 1.7 s but **min ≈ 0.21 s** — spikes are not observed after `stop_time`, so the tail of the −2.5→+1.5 s window is unobserved in a minority of trials (same in the reference pipeline, which used a −3→+3 s window on the same per-trial data).\n    97→- Last `sample_start` before the go cue is ~1.85–1.95 s before the go cue (tone onset); this matches the paper's sample epoch start at −1.85 s and 1.2 s delay.\n    98→- Tongue `likelihood` is strongly bimodal (≈ 89 % < 0.1, ≈ 10 % > 0.9) → a likelihood threshold cleanly defines tongue visibility.\n    99→\n   100→---\n   101→\n   102→## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n   103→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   104→\n   105→Sources: `/app/methods.txt` (STAR Methods excerpt of the data paper), `/app/datapaper.pdf` (Chen et al. 2024, *Cell* — \"Brain-wide neural activity underlying memory-guided movement\"), `/app/methodpaper.pdf` (Wang*, Kurgyis* et al. 2025, *Nat Neurosci*), `/app/ChenLiuEtAl2023_SpikeSortingQC.pdf`. Text extracted to `/app/cache/*.txt` with `pypdf`.\n   106→\n   107→### Expected Statistics (from papers/methods)\n   108→| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n   109→|-----------|-------|--------------|\n   110→| Good units (total) | 69,943 | \"the dataset consisted of 69,943 good units recorded across 173 behavioral sessions\" (methods.txt) |\n   111→| Sessions | 173 | same quote; \"660 penetrations\" (Fig. 1J) |\n   114→| Fraction of Kilosort2 clusters kept | 25.9 % | \"This corresponds to 25.9 % of clusters reported by Kilosort2\" |\n   115→| Good units by area | ALM 8,717; striatum 7,664; thalamus 12,808; midbrain 7,495; medulla 2,928 | methods.txt |\n   116→| Trials / session | mean 476, range 130–785 | \"Mice performed on average 476 (Mean; range, 130-785) trials per session\" |\n   117→| Correct rate | 84 % (range 65–99 %) | same sentence |\n   118→| Session inclusion | performance > 65 %, ≥ 50 correct lick-left and ≥ 50 correct lick-right trials | \"We selected experimental sessions for analysis based on following criteria...\" |\n   119→| Photostim trials | ~25 % randomly interleaved, 5 mW/hemisphere, last 0.5 s of delay, N = 17 VGAT-ChR2 mice | \"Photoinhibition ... deployed on a subset of ~25% randomly interleaved trials\" |\n   120→| Photostim effect | performance 83.2 % → 71.7 % (bilateral ALM) | methods.txt |\n   121→| Neural data time bin (method paper) | 40 ms width, 3.4 ms stride | \"we binned spikes into firing rates with a bin width of 40 ms and a stride of 3.4 ms\" |\n   122→| Behavior/video sampling | 300 Hz cameras (data timestamps dt = 3.4 ms) | \"High-speed videos ... acquired at 300 Hz\" |\n   123→| Task epochs | sample tones 3×150 ms with 100 ms gaps (≈0.65 s), delay 1.2 s, go cue 0.1 s (6 kHz), answer 1.5 s | methods.txt Behavior section |\n   124→| Method-paper session count used | 105–106 sessions (video analyses) | \"n = 106 sessions\" |\n   125→\n   126→### Processing Details\n   127→- **Alignment**: everything aligned to the **go cue** (t = 0) in both papers; reference epochs sample [−1.85, −1.2] s, delay [−1.2, 0] s, response [0, 1.5] s.\n   128→- **Binning**: sliding 40 ms window, 3.4 ms stride, rates in Hz (spikes/bin_width). Our task specifies **50 ms bins**, which we implement as non-overlapping 50 ms bins giving rates in Hz (80 bins over −2.5→+1.5 s).\n   129→- **Spike sorting / QC**: Kilosort2 output; 15 quality metrics; five region-specific logistic-regression classifiers (cortex, striatum, thalamus, midbrain, medulla) trained on manual Phy labels; units labelled **`good`** are used for all analyses. In the NWB files this classifier output is the `units.classification` column (`good` vs `unlabelled`), and `anno_name` holds the CCF annotation of classified units. The whitepaper explicitly argues that thresholding individual metrics is inadequate — hence using the provided classifier label is the faithful reproduction of their QC.\n   130→- **Video markers**: DeepLabCut side-view tongue/jaw/nose; outliers detected by 5-sigma velocity threshold and imputed from nearby frames; when the tongue is occluded (in the mouth) the method paper sets tongue position to its mean value. Our decoder task instead requires an explicit \"not visible\" class (class 3), so occlusion is represented as its own category (detected via DLC likelihood).\n   131→- **Region grouping** (reference preprocessing loop): ALM, Medulla, Midbrain, Striatum, Thalamus, Pons, Cerebellum, Hypothalamus, Hippocampus, Orbital, OtherCortex, Olfactory, CorticalSubplate, Pallidum — each split into left/right hemisphere with the CCF ML midline at 5,700 µm.\n   132→\n   133→### Curation Steps\n   134→**Neuron curation rules (reference)**\n   135→1. Keep only units with a CCF/histology annotation (units without histology are dropped).\n   136→2. Keep only units labelled `good` by the QC classifier (25.9 % of Kilosort clusters).\n   137→3. Method-paper analyses additionally drop neurons with mean firing rate < 2 Hz (for firing-rate *prediction* analyses) and brain areas with < 10 neurons per session. These are analysis-specific and are **not** appropriate for the decoding task here (they would discard most of the population available to the decoder), so they are not applied; documented in Step 5.\n   138→\n   139→**Trial curation rules (reference)**\n   140→- \"regular\" trials = no early lick, no auto-water, no free water, no ignore (`correctness != -1`), and no photostimulation.\n   141→- **Our decoder task requires early-lick, photostim, ignore and miss trials** (they are explicit decoder inputs/outputs), so these filters are relaxed; see Step 5.\n   142→- Session inclusion criteria in the data paper (performance > 65 %, ≥ 50 correct left and right trials) are already satisfied by the published sessions.\n   143→\n   144→### Decoders Trained (reference accuracies)\n   145→| Decoded variable | Accuracy |\n   146→|---|---|\n   147→| Choice from behavioral video, pre-sample epoch | AUC 0.51 ± 0.06 (n = 106 sessions) |\n   148→| Choice from behavioral video, sample+delay epochs | AUC 0.66 ± 0.12 |\n   149→| Choice from behavioral video, second half of response epoch | AUC 0.99 ± 0.01 |\n   150→| Choice from single-neuron delay activity (AUC threshold for \"choice-modulated\") | AUC > 0.65 |\n   151→| Uninstructed-movement type from single neurons | AUC > 0.65 threshold |\n   152→\n   153→Implication for our decoder: **choice** should be decodable near-perfectly after the go cue from population activity (video alone gives AUC 0.99); delay-epoch choice information is weaker. Outcome and early-lick should be decodable well above chance; tongue position (a directly observable movement) should be decodable well, since neural activity predicts video markers with high R² in the method paper.\n   154→\n   155→---\n   156→\n   157→## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   158→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   159→\n   160→I cross-checked the reference code (Step 1), the NWB data (Step 2) and the papers (Step 3). Session-selection criteria (>65 % performance) are approximately satisfied; 16/174 sessions are slightly below by my computation but are part of the published dataset, so no session is dropped for performance. |\n   172→| Photostim | `stimulation[:,0] != 0` marks stim trials | 18,588/94,990 trials (19.6 %) at 5.5 mW, always 0.5 s; onset −1.2 s or −0.5 s re go cue | \"~25 % randomly interleaved trials\", 5 mW/hemisphere, last 0.5 s of delay | Consistent (5.5 mW commanded ≈ 5 mW delivered; some sessions have no stim, lowering the overall fraction). Auto-water and free-water trials (reward independent of the animal's action, 4 % of trials) are **excluded**, matching the reference, since their `outcome` label is not behaviourally meaningful. |\n   175→| Choice definition | `trial_type` (instruction) + `correctness` | `outcome` + `trial_instruction` reproduces the lick-derived choice in >99 % of trials (disagreements are `ignore` trials with a lick after the response window) | hit = licked instructed side, miss = licked other side, ignore = no lick | Choice := instructed side for `hit`, opposite side for `miss`, `no lick` for `ignore`. |\n   176→| Binning | 40 ms window / 3.4 ms stride (sliding) | spike times available at full resolution | same | Task specifies **50 ms bins** → non-overlapping 50 ms bins, rates in Hz (counts/0.05 s), 80 bins for −2.5→+1.5 s. |\n   177→| Observation window | spike times truncated to [−3, 3] s re go cue | `obs_intervals` == trial (start_time, stop_time); **all** spikes lie inside trials; 96.9 % of trials cover go−2.5 s and 84.4 % cover go+1.5 s (miss trials terminate ~0.8 s after the go cue) | n/a | Bins outside the trial's observed interval contain no spikes by construction; they are set to 0 and flagged in metadata. Dropping short trials is not an option (it would delete 95 % of `miss` trials and destroy the outcome output). |\n   178→\n   179→---\n   180→\n   181→## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n   182→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   183→\n   184→### Variable Mapping\n   185→| Source (NWB) | Target field | Transform | Reference-code counterpart | Notes |\n   186→|---|---|---|---|---|\n   187→| `units.spike_times` (session clock) for units with `classification == 'good'` | `neural[session][trial]` (n_neurons, 80) | subtract that trial's go-cue time; for our task we are instructed to use 50-ms bins, so we will use non-overlapping 50-ms bins (stride = width = 50 ms), which is the natural analogue.\n- Epochs used in the paper (relative to go cue): sample `[-1.85, -1.2]` s, delay `[-1.2, 0]` s, response `[0, 1.5]` s. So the trial structure is ~1.3 s sample + 1.2 s delay; our window of -2.5 to +1.5 s around the go cue covers pre-sample, sample, delay and response.\n- Neuron QC in the reference is performed with an **external classifier-based good-unit list** (`goodunits/*.mat`) not shipped with the NWB data. The NWB `units` table (DANDI 000363) contains per-unit QC metrics (`unit_amp`, `presence_ratio`, `amplitude_cutoff`, `isi_violation`, `drift_metric`, `unit_quality`...) and typically a `unit_quality`/`quality` field; we will reproduce the QC using the criteria in the Chen/Liu et al. 2023 spike-sorting QC white paper (to be read in Step 3) applied to those metrics.\n- Units without CCF histology annotation are dropped in the reference (`unit_comb = set(ephys unit ids) & set(histology unit ids)`).\n- Hemisphere assignment: CCF ML midline at 5700 µm; `ccf_x >= 5700` = left hemisphere.\n- Trial curation for the reference **regression** analyses excludes early-lick / auto-water / free-water / no-response / photostim trials. Our decoder task explicitly requires photostim as an input and early lick, no-lick choice and ignore/miss outcome as outputs, so those trials must be **kept**; we will only exclude auto-water/free-water trials (not real behaviour) if warranted, documented in Step 5.\n\n---\n\n## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Data Structure\n- `/app/data/` contains one directory per mouse: `sub-<subject_id>/` (28 subjects) and `dandiset.yaml` (DANDI:000363, Mesoscale Activity Map dataset, Chen et al.).\n- Each session is one NWB file: `sub-<id>_ses-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSS>_behavior+ecephys+ogen.nwb`; **174 files total**.\n- All files have identical trial and unit column sets (verified across all 174 files).\n\n**NWB contents (read with `pynwb.NWBHDF5IO`):**\n- `nwb.subject.subject_id` (e.g. `440956`), `nwb.subject.description` = lab mouse name (e.g. `SC015`); onset relative to **trial start**), `trial_uid`, `task` (all `audio delay`), `task_protocol` (all 1), `trial_instruction` (`left`/`right`), `early_lick` (`early`/`no early`), `outcome` (`hit`/`miss`/`ignore`), `auto_water` (0/1), `free_water` (0/1).\n- `nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents']` (`BehavioralEvents`), each a `TimeSeries` whose **timestamps** carry the event times (session clock, seconds):\n  `presample_start/stop_times`, `sample_start/stop_times`, `delay_start/stop_times`, `go_start/stop_times` (n == n_trials in **all** 174 sessions), `trialend_start/stop_times`, `left_lick_times`, `right_lick_times`, `photostim_start_times` (data = laser power in mW), `photostim_stop_times`.\n  Note `sample_*` and `delay_*` have **more** events than trials because early licking triggers a **replay** of the sample/delay epoch (confirmed: extra sample events occur only in `early` trials).\n- `nwb.acquisition['BehavioralTimeSeries']`: DeepLabCut side-view (`Camera0_side_`) tracking `JawTracking`, `NoseTracking`, `TongueTracking` (all 174 sessions), occasionally `WhiskerTracking_whisker` (20), `LickPortTracking` (4) and bottom-camera (`Camera3_bottom_*`) series. `trial stop - go`: median ≈ 1.7 s but **min ≈ 0.21 s** — spikes are not observed after `stop_time`, so the tail of the −2.5→+1.5 s window is unobserved in a minority of trials (same in the reference pipeline, which used a −3→+3 s window on the same per-trial data).\n- Last `sample_start` before the go cue is ~1.85–1.95 s before the go cue (tone onset); reference epochs sample [−1.85, −1.2] s, delay [−1.2, 0] s, response [0, 1.5] s.\n- **Binning**: sliding 40 ms window, 3.4 ms stride, rates in Hz (spikes/bin_width). Our task specifies **50 ms bins**, which we implement as non-overlapping 50 ms bins giving rates in Hz (80 bins over −2.5→+1.5 s).\n- **Spike sorting / QC**: Kilosort2 output; 15 quality metrics; five region-specific logistic-regression classifiers (cortex, striatum, thalamus, midbrain, medulla) trained on manual Phy labels; units labelled **`good`** are used for all analyses. In the NWB files this classifier output is the `units.classification` column (`good` vs `unlabelled`), and `anno_name` holds the CCF annotation of classified units. The whitepaper explicitly argues that thresholding individual metrics is inadequate — hence using the provided classifier label is the faithful reproduction of their QC.\n- **Video markers**: DeepLabCut side-view tongue/jaw/nose; outliers detected by 5-sigma velocity threshold and imputed from nearby frames; when the tongue is occluded (in the mouth) the method paper sets tongue position to its mean value. Our decoder task instead requires an explicit \"not visible\" class (class 3), so occlusion is represented as its own category (detected via DLC likelihood).\n- **Region grouping** (reference preprocessing loop): ALM, Medulla, Midbrain, Striatum, Thalamus, Pons, Cerebellum, Hypothalamus, Hippocampus, Orbital, OtherCortex, Olfactory, CorticalSubplate, Pallidum — each split into left/right hemisphere with the CCF ML midline at 5,700 µm.\n\n### Curation Steps\n**Neuron curation rules (reference)**\n1. Keep only units with a CCF/histology annotation (units without histology are dropped).\n2. Keep only units labelled `good` by the QC classifier (25.9 % of Kilosort clusters).\n3. Method-paper analyses additionally drop neurons with mean firing rate < 2 Hz (for firing-rate *prediction* analyses) and brain areas with < 10 neurons per session. These are analysis-specific and are **not** appropriate for the decoding task here (they would discard most of the population available to the decoder), so they are not applied; documented in Step 5.\n\n**Trial curation rules (reference)**\n- \"regular\" trials = no early lick, no auto-water, no free water, no ignore (`correctness != -1`), and no photostimulation.\n- **Our decoder task requires early-lick, photostim, ignore and miss trials** (they are explicit decoder inputs/outputs), so these filters are relaxed; see Step 5.\n- Session inclusion criteria in the data paper (performance > 65 %, ≥ 50 correct left and right trials) are already satisfied by the published sessions.\n\n### Decoders Trained (reference accuracies)\n| Decoded variable | Accuracy |\n|---|---|\n| Choice from behavioral video, pre-sample epoch | AUC 0.51 ± 0.06 (n = 106 sessions) |\n| Choice from behavioral video, sample+delay epochs | AUC 0.66 ± 0.12 |\n| Choice from behavioral video, second half of response epoch | AUC 0.99 ± 0.01 |\n| Choice from single-neuron delay activity (AUC threshold for \"choice-modulated\") | AUC > 0.65 |\n| Uninstructed-movement type from single neurons | AUC > 0.65 threshold |\n\nImplication for our decoder: **choice** should be decodable near-perfectly after the go cue from population activity (video alone gives AUC 0.99); delay-epoch choice information is weaker. Outcome and early-lick should be decodable well above chance; tongue position (a directly observable movement) should be decodable well, since neural activity predicts video markers with high R² in the method paper.\n\n---\n\n## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nI cross-checked the reference code (Step 1), the NWB data (Step 2) and the papers (Step 3). Scripts: `/app/cache/consistency*.py`, `/app/cache/region_map.py`, `/app/cache/regions*.py`, `/app/cache/timing_check.py`, `/app/cache/coverage.py`, `/app/cache/choice_check.py`.\n\n### Discrepancies Found and Resolved\n| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Papers say | Resolution |\n|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n| File format | `.mat` DataJoint exports (`loadmat`) | NWB (DANDI 000363) | same dataset | The NWB release contains all fields used by the reference `.mat` pipeline (trials, events, units + QC classifications, CCF annotations, DLC tracking). Use `pynwb`; map each `.mat` field to its NWB equivalent (table below in Step 5). |\n| Neuron QC | External classifier good-unit lists (`goodunits/*.mat`) | `units.classification` ∈ {`good`, `unlabelled`}; only `good` units carry `anno_name` (CCF annotation) | \"Applying the trained classifiers ... provided lists of units that were labeled as 'good' and were used for the analysis\" | `classification == 'good'` **is** the shipped output of that classifier. rates = counts / 0.05 s.\n   231→- `tone_onset_times()` — last `sample_start_times` at/ before the go cue per trial (handles early-lick epoch replays).\n   232→- `photostim_binary()` — binary per-bin photostim indicator from `photostim_start/stop_times` events (bin counted as on if it overlaps the laser interval).\n   233→- `bin_tongue()` — DLC side-camera tongue y averaged per 50 ms bin over frames with `likelihood > 0.5`, after 5σ velocity outlier rejection (method paper); NaN when no valid frame.\n   234→- `discretize_tongue()` — per-session 40th/60th percentiles of the binned visible y → classes 0/1/2, NaN → 3.\n   235→- `convert_session()` — loads one NWB with `pynwb`, curates units/trials, builds neural/input/output arrays and a `session_info` record with timings and QC statistics.\n   236→- `make_processing_plots()` — 8-panel diagnostics per session (`--show-processing`): raw spike raster vs binned rates for one trial, population PSTH computed independently from raw spike times vs the converted neural mean, inputs vs raw event times, raw tongue trace vs binned values and percentile thresholds, resulting discrete tongue classes, per-trial outputs, tongue-class time course.\n   237→- `main()` — parallel over sessions (`ProcessPoolExecutor`, default 16 workers), assembles the target dictionary, writes the pickle, prints summary statistics.\n   238→\n   239→Efficiency:\n   240→- Spike times are read once per unit; binning is vectorised with `searchsorted` over concatenated bin edges (0.16 s/session for ~450 neurons × 400 trials).\n   241→- Unit → electrode lookup uses the `VectorIndex` arrays directly instead of per-unit `units['electrodes'][i]` DataFrame construction (~100× faster; verified to give identical electrode ids).\n   242→- Video tracking arrays are read once per session and binned with `np.bincount`.\n   243→- Sessions are processed in parallel (16 workers), so the wall-clock cost is dominated by pickling the ~10 GB result.\n   244→\n   245→Issue found and fixed during development: **trials with no ephys at all**. In 100 sessions some trials (4.35 % overall; 2 sessions where the probes stop long before the behaviour: 376/582 and 321/480 trials) contain zero spikes from *every* good unit over the whole trial. NWB does not flag them (`is_good_trials` is all-True and `obs_intervals` simply mirror the trials table). Such trials are excluded as invalid data periods.\n   246→\n   247→---\n   248→\n   249→## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n   250→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   251→\n   252→`python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing` → `/app/conversion_sample_out.txt`;\n   253→`python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/sample_data.pkl --verify-only` → `/app/verification_sample_out.txt` (**no errors, no warnings**).\n   254→\n   255→### Sample Statistics (2 sessions of mouse 440956 / SC015)\n   256→| Statistic | Value |\n   257→|-----------|-------|\n   258→| Sessions | 2 |\n   259→| Neurons (total) | 834 |\n   260→| Neurons / session | 459, 375 |\n   261→| Subjects | 1 |\n   262→| Trials (total) | 513 (of 834 raw; Not applied (documented deviation).\n3. **Session curation**: drop sessions with 0 good units → exactly **173** sessions, matching the papers.\n4. **Trial curation**: drop `auto_water == 1` or `free_water == 1` trials (reward delivered independent of the animal's action ⇒ `outcome` is not a behavioural report), matching `get_regular_trial_mask`. **Keep** early-lick, ignore/miss and photostim trials, because the decoder task defines them as outputs/inputs; the reference excluded them only for its encoding analyses.\n5. **Alignment / window / binning**: go-cue aligned, −2.5 → +1.5 s, 80 × 50 ms non-overlapping bins, rates in Hz (counts / 0.05 s). Bin *i* covers [−2.5 + 0.05 i, −2.45 + 0.05 i); the value assigned to input/output time series uses the **bin centre**.\n6. **Unobserved time**: spikes exist only inside `[start_time, stop_time]` (= `obs_intervals`). 96.9 % of trials cover go−2.5 s, 84.4 % cover go+1.5 s (miss trials end early). Bins outside the recorded trial interval necessarily contain 0 spikes; these are kept as 0 (no extra information is available) and the fraction is reported in metadata.\n7. **Tongue visibility threshold**: `likelihood > 0.5`. The likelihood distribution is strongly bimodal (89 % < 0.1, 10 % > 0.9) so the exact threshold is immaterial.\n8. **Percentiles computed per session** over the *binned, visible* tongue-y values of the exported trials, so the resulting class frequencies are exactly 40 / 20 / 40 % of visible bins in each session.\n9. **Outputs are time-varying** (shape (4, 80)): per-trial variables are broadcast across bins, as recommended by the task description.\n10. **Brain regions carry hemisphere** (e.g. `left ALM`), as in the reference (`side_region`), with the CCF ML midline at 5,700 µm.\n\n### Planned Sanity Checks\n- [ ] 173 sessions, 28 subjects, 69,453 neurons after curation.\n- [ ] Per-coarse-region neuron totals reproduce paper values (striatum 7,664; rates = counts / 0.05 s.\n- `tone_onset_times()` — last `sample_start_times` at/ before the go cue per trial (handles early-lick epoch replays).\n- `photostim_binary()` — binary per-bin photostim indicator from `photostim_start/stop_times` events (bin counted as on if it overlaps the laser interval).\n- `bin_tongue()` — DLC side-camera tongue y averaged per 50 ms bin over frames with `likelihood > 0.5`, after 5σ velocity outlier rejection (method paper); NaN when no valid frame.\n- `discretize_tongue()` — per-session 40th/60th percentiles of the binned visible y → classes 0/1/2, NaN → 3.\n- `convert_session()` — loads one NWB with `pynwb`, curates units/trials, builds neural/input/output arrays and a `session_info` record with timings and QC statistics.\n- `make_processing_plots()` — 8-panel diagnostics per session (`--show-processing`): raw spike raster vs binned rates for one trial, population PSTH computed independently from raw spike times vs the converted neural mean, inputs vs raw event times, raw tongue trace vs binned values and percentile thresholds, resulting discrete tongue classes, per-trial outputs, tongue-class time course.\n- `main()` — parallel over sessions (`ProcessPoolExecutor`, default 16 workers), assembles the target dictionary, writes the pickle, prints summary statistics.\n\nEfficiency:\n- Spike times are read once per unit; binning is vectorised with `searchsorted` over concatenated bin edges (0.16 s/session for ~450 neurons × 400 trials).\n- Unit → electrode lookup uses the `VectorIndex` arrays directly instead of per-unit `units['electrodes'][i]` DataFrame construction (~100× faster; verified to give identical electrode ids).\n- Video tracking arrays are read once per session and binned with `np.bincount`.\n- Sessions are processed in parallel (16 workers), so the wall-clock cost is dominated by pickling the ~10 GB result.\n\nIssue found and fixed during development: **trials with no ephys at all**. In 100 sessions some trials (4.35 % overall; 2 sessions where the probes stop long before the behaviour: 376/582 and 321/480 trials) contain zero spikes from *every* good unit over the whole trial. NWB does not flag them (`is_good_trials` is all-True and `obs_intervals` simply mirror the trials table). Spike times are binned into 80 non-overlapping 50 ms bins spanning -2.5 to +1.5 s relative to the go cue, using `np.searchsorted` over flattened bin edges for all trials. Spike counts are divided by the bin width (0.05 s) to get firing rates in Hz. No smoothing, normalization, or baseline subtraction is applied.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef bin_spikes(spike_times_list, go_times):\n    edges = (go_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES_REL[None, :]).ravel()\n    out = np.empty((len(spike_times_list), ntr, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    for k, st in enumerate(spike_times_list):\n        idx = np.searchsorted(st, edges).reshape(ntr, NBINS + 1)\n        out[k] = np.diff(idx, axis=1)\n    out /= np.float32(BIN_SIZE)\n    return out\n```\n\niii. Is any temporal rebinning applied?\n\ni. 50 ms non-overlapping bins, 80 bins total from -2.5 s to +1.5 s relative to the go cue. What variables in the raw data is `input` *Time from tone onset in seconds* derived from?\n\ni. From `BehavioralEvents/sample_start_times` (tone onset timestamps) and `go_start_times` (go cue times). Alignment is guaranteed by construction since both use the same temporal grid.\n\n## 4-a. What variables in the raw data is `input` *Photostimulation* derived from?\n\ni. Stimulation events are assigned to trials by `searchsorted` on trial start times. For each event, the onset and offset are expressed relative to the trial's go cue. A bin is marked 1 if the stimulation interval overlaps the bin (using bin edges), else 0. This produces a binary time-varying input.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef photostim_binary(stim_on, stim_off, trial_starts, go_times):\n    ps = np.zeros((ntr, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    tidx = np.searchsorted(trial_starts, stim_on, side='right') - 1\n    for i, on, off in zip(tidx, stim_on, stim_off):\n        on_rel, off_rel = on - go_times[i], off - go_times[i]\n        overlap = (BIN_EDGES_REL[:-1] < off_rel) & (BIN_EDGES_REL[1:] > on_rel)\n        ps[i, overlap] = 1.0\n    return ps\n```\n\niii. The AI notes this approach detects any overlap between the stimulation interval and each bin, rather than checking if the bin center falls within the interval.\n\n## 4-c. How is `input` *Photostimulation* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. The onset and offset are expressed relative to the go cue, and compared against the go-cue-relative bin edges, ensuring alignment with the neural data grid.\n\nii.\n```python\non_rel, off_rel = on - go_times[i], off - go_times[i]\noverlap = (BIN_EDGES_REL[:-1] < off_rel) & (BIN_EDGES_REL[1:] > on_rel)\n```\n\niii. Same temporal reference frame as neural data.\n\n## 5-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Lick direction choice* derived from?\n\ni. Per-trial constant, broadcast across all 80 bins.\n\nii.\n```python\noutcome_code = np.where(out_k == 'ignore', 0, np.where(out_k == 'miss', 1, 2)).astype(np.int64)\n```\n\niii.
